Selftest GREEN (3 cases + app==awk crux), TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/network/custody. **The Constel dev-net harness stands complete end to end -- r1 named genesis - r2 the fractal settles - r3 the net travels - r4 reads true.** The one remaining harness horizon rung -- **Comlink-served** (a constel shared over the wire) -- reaches the serve custody gate (Keaton's hand). **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D journey worth a self-approved design read -- the dev-net now stands as a bench, so the natural next crux is a real Comlink handshake or settlement test *running against a spun-up constel* (proving the harness earns its keep), or the Mycelium consensus design read (over TigerBeetle+Mysticeti). The three breach renames (Dimeroll->Dimeroll - .myc->.kres - council sky->constel sky, each cairn-first) and the deep debride + seed force-push stay Keaton's hand (gates #1/#5).

- **Constel dev-net ledger r1 LANDED `20260813`** (`active-designing/20260813-030005_constel-devnet-ledger-exploration.md`, self-approved) -- the harness's own named next crux, taken: a real settlement/economic test *running against a spun-up constel*, proving the bench earns its keep. `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger.rye` gives the tree's real Mycelium supply primitive (`mycelium/fold.rye` -- supply as a pure fold over Kumara-signed facts, `Sigma issued - Sigma taxed`, non-negative, an overdraw refusing the whole fold) **a network to belong to**. The crux, fused: **an economic ledger runs from genesis inside the fake net -- its money signed by the net's own settled identity, refused for anyone else -- and it can never run under a name that could be real.** Two promises hold at once: the **quarantine wraps the ledger, not only the net** (`open_ledger` opens only through `spin_up`, which refuses `UnsafeName` before a single genesis -- no code path to a ledger under an @p-shaped name), and **sovereignty is structural** (`admit` refuses any fact whose signer's key is not the genesis galaxy Deed's `keeper` -> `NotSovereign` before the log; `issue`/`tax` mint sovereign-signed facts, the sovereign keypair derived from the same keeper seed that settled genesis, asserted equal to `galaxy.keeper` -- settlement identity gates the Mycelium supply, neither module weakened). Selftest GREEN (5 cases): a law-safe net opens a ledger whose sovereign is the genesis keeper (fresh supply 0) - issue 100 / tax 30 / issue 20 folds to supply 90 over three sovereign facts - an @p-shaped name opens **no** ledger (`UnsafeName`) - a tax of 80 over supply 50 refuses `Overdraw` whole (supply held at 50, log untouched -- Mycelium non-negativity holds in the fake net) - an alien signer's internally-honest fact refuses `NotSovereign` before the log (log stays empty). Witness `tools/constel_net_ledger_witness.rish` GREEN, re-runs the harness (`constel_net`) **and** the Mycelium fold at its door; TAME bans + width clean. Composes `constel_net.spin_up` + `mycelium/fold` public API only, invents no storage, weakens no bound. Demo keeper seeds only -- the Mycelium fold is a supply *model*, not a payment rail (gate #3 untouched), no real key/funds/network/custody (gate #4 untouched; a served ledger reaches the serve gate). **Constel dev-net ledger r2 LANDED `20260813`:** `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger_stars.rye` carries the **third Mycelium fact kind -- `star_reserve`** -- into the sovereign ledger, mirroring the harness's own r2 (a star + planet settling beneath the galaxy): the ledger's *names* grow beside the topology's *points*, every reservation paid out of the net's own supply. `reserve_star` mints a sovereign-signed reservation and admits it; a reservation is **sovereign-only** (`NotSovereign` before the log), **unique** (a second reservation of a held name refuses `StarTaken` whole, supply held), and **drains supply exactly as tax does** (a reservation priced beyond supply refuses `Overdraw`, non-negativity holding). Additive -- r1 grew only a pub reader `signer_is_sovereign_of` (the exact private gate `admit` uses, so one authority test never drifts into two); r1 re-runs GREEN at r2's door. Selftest GREEN (4 cases: issue 100 then reserve vega(30)+rigel(20) drains to 50 - double-vega refuses StarTaken - overpriced refuses Overdraw - alien reservation refuses NotSovereign). Witness `tools/constel_net_ledger_stars_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/funds/network/custody. **Constel dev-net ledger r3 LANDED `20260813`:** `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger_bron.rye` makes the ledger **travel as text** -- `from_ledger` builds a `PortableLedger` (owned name buffer - sovereign key - roster of signed facts) from a live ledger; `render` emits a `format constel-ledger-v1` record (a `net <name>` line - a `sovereign <pk-hex>` line - one `fact <kind> <amount> <star> <sig-hex>` line each); `parse` reads it back and `supply` re-folds the reconstructed facts through the real Mycelium law. Two promises ride in the bytes, distinct from the harness's number-only r3: the **quarantine travels** (a record whose net name carries no digit refuses `UnsafeName`, exactly as `open_ledger` refuses one at genesis) and the **sovereignty travels** (each fact binds the carried sovereign key as its signer, and the fold re-verifies every signature against it -- a record whose `sovereign` line does not match its facts refuses `IdentityRefused`; the sovereign key is load-bearing in the bytes, not a note). Round-trip proven: recovers safe name + sovereign key + supply 50 over three facts, re-render byte-for-byte identical (a fixed point). Purpose-built bounded flat-Bron reader: unknown field - bad header - missing net - missing sovereign - bad hex each refuse. One build red caught same-round (the tokens buffer was sized [4] but a fact line carries five tokens -> grew to [5]). Composes r1 + r2 public API (r2 re-runs GREEN at r3's door). Selftest GREEN (8 cases). Witness `tools/constel_net_ledger_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/funds/network/custody. **Constel dev-net ledger r4 LANDED `20260813` -- the ledger's agent-doable arc CLOSES:** `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger_true.rye` carries the reader onto a **real on-disk ledger record** -- `tools/fixtures/constel_net_ledger.bron`, produced reproducibly by `pond/apps/constel_net_ledger_fixture_gen.rye` (issue 100 - tax 30 - reserve vega 20 under `xnqvel34`, supply 50), `@embedFile`-bound through a `pond/apps/corpora/` symlink. The reader parses through r3, **re-folds** the record through the real Mycelium law (so the reported supply is the law's own fold, its signatures re-verified against the carried sovereign key), and reports **fact count - supply - reservations**; the witness asserts that triple equals `tools/fixtures/constel_net_ledger_truth.awk`'s reading (`3 50 1`, two tools one answer -- a signature-verifying fold vs a bare arithmetic sum agreeing). Two genuineness proofs beyond the triple: the generator re-emits byte-identical bytes (not a hand-mock) and re-rendering the parsed ledger reproduces the embedded bytes exactly, the recovered name still law-safe; a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Composes r3 public API (re-runs GREEN at r4's door). Selftest GREEN (3 cases + measure). Witness `tools/constel_net_ledger_true_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds only, no key/funds/network/custody. **The Constel dev-net ledger stands complete end to end -- r1 sovereign genesis - r2 star reservations - r3 travels as text - r4 reads true.** The one remaining ledger horizon rung -- **Comlink-served** -- reaches the serve custody gate (Keaton's hand). **Next agent-doable crux:** the Mycelium consensus design read (over TigerBeetle+Mysticeti) now that a fake net runs the real supply law end to end, or a fresh Season D journey worth a self-approved design read. Breach renames + deep debride stay Keaton's hand (gates #1/#5).

- **Mycelium consensus -- the Cord OPENED `20260813`** (Season D; design read `active-designing/20260813-032851_mycelium-consensus-cord-exploration.md`, self-approved). With the constel dev-net ledger closed, REMEMBER's live edge named the next Lindy-first crux plainly -- the Mycelium consensus design read over TigerBeetle + Mysticeti. The design read fixed the shape, the name, and the four crux-first rounds: from **TigerBeetle**, determinism + static allocation (mycelium/fold already keeps this -- a pure fold over signed facts, static log 1024); from **Mysticeti** (Mysten Labs, Bullshark/Narwhal lineage, studied clean-room under Sui's permissive license), order read from an **uncertified DAG** by a deterministic commit rule, no leader, no certificate round. The synthesis earns its own clear name per `comlink-tendency` -- **Weave** is already Mantra's text-history CRDT, so the consensus-ordering layer is **Cord** (a mycelial rhizomorph: bundled hyphae transporting as one; grepped zero collisions). Four rounds: **r1** deterministic commit - **r2** Byzantine refusal (equivocation, cycle, f<n/3) - **r3** travels as `format cord-dag-v1` Bron - **r4** read a real fixture true; the *improving-on-both* aim named as a **horizon**, not a witnessed benchmark (a later `loom` round). Also gitignored `/mycelium/bin/` (build artifact). **Cord r1 LANDED `20260813` -- the crux CLOSED:** `mycelium/cord.rye` builds a bounded Dag of Kumara-signed Blocks (author key - round - parent hashes - one `mycelium/fold` Fact - author signature over canonical bytes); `add` verifies the block signature and admits a block only when **every parent hash it names is already held** (so the graph is acyclic by construction -- a block may only point backward), refusing `BadSignature` - `DanglingParent` - `TooManyParents` - `DagFull` - `Duplicate` whole; `commit` is a **pure canonical topological walk** (round asc, then author key, then block hash) yielding one total order over which `fold_log` runs unchanged. The crux proven on metal: **four blocks added in three different arrival permutations commit to one byte-identical order and one identical fold state** (arrival-order independence -- the Mysticeti insight under TigerBeetle bounds), the agreed order folding the real Mycelium law to supply 700 over one star; a tampered block signature, a dangling parent, and a duplicate each refuse before the Dag grows. Selftest GREEN first pass; witness `tools/mycelium_cord_witness.rish` GREEN (re-runs the Mycelium fold at its door), TAME bans clean, width-check exit 0. Composes public API only (mycelium/fold + tally/kumara), invents no storage, weakens no bound. Demo keeper seeds only -- no key/funds/network/custody; a Cord gossiped over the wire reaches the serve gate. **Cord r2 LANDED `20260813` -- Byzantine refusal:** the adversary a leaderless DAG must survive is **equivocation** -- one author publishing two distinct blocks at the same round, hoping honest nodes admit different ones and split history. The Cord quarantines it by admission control (an author speaks at most once per round). Additive -- r1's `add` stays a green binary; r2 is `mycelium/cord_byzantine.rye` composing r1 public API; `cord.rye` grew only two small pubs (`address` -- a block's SHA-256, telling a same-hash `Duplicate` from a different-hash equivocation; `verify_block` -- the signature check extracted so `add`/`add_bft` share one crypto check), r1 re-running GREEN. `add_bft` rejects a forgery (`BadSignature`) **before** classifying an equivocation (`Equivocation`), then delegates to r1's `add`. **The r2 crux on metal:** the honest four-block commit order + fold state are **byte-identical with a quarantined equivocator** (author A's second distinct genesis at round 0 refused, the Dag unchanged, commit re-run identical -- supply 700, one star). Supporting: the same author at a **later** round is admitted (per-round, not per-author); a forward reference refuses `DanglingParent` (a block points only backward -- no cycle); a forgery refuses `BadSignature`, never mislabeled. The `f<n/3` framing honored as a **mechanism** (safety by admission control), a simulated benchmark a named later `loom` round (Two Rooms). GREEN first pass; witness `tools/mycelium_cord_byzantine_witness.rish` GREEN (re-runs r1 at its door), TAME + width clean. **Cord r3 LANDED `20260813` -- the Cord travels:** `mycelium/cord_bron.rye` renders a committed Dag to a `format cord-dag-v1` record (one signed `block` line each in committed order -- author key - round - fact - fact signature - block signature -- parents as following `parent <hash>` lines) and parses it back. The whole block rides (both signatures + every fact field; star/body hex-encoded with a `-` sentinel -- Cord facts carry memos as bodies), so the recovered block is byte-identical. **The r3 crux -- the agreed ORDER travels with the bytes:** the recovered Dag, re-admitted under r1's own `add` (every signature re-verified, every parent already held), commits to the **byte-identical order** and folds the identical supply (700, one star) -- two nodes agree by exchanging blocks alone. Re-rendering reproduces the exact bytes (fixed point); a tampered block refuses `BadSignature` on rebuild -- corruption cannot cross as consensus. Bounded flat-Bron reader refuses `BadFormat`-`UnknownField`-`BadLine`-`BadInt`-`BadHex`-`ParentBeforeBlock`-`TooManyParents`-`TooManyBlocks`. Added a `mycelium/parse_int.rye` symlink (matching the kumara/tally_copy pattern). GREEN first pass; witness `tools/mycelium_cord_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (re-runs r1 and r2), TAME + width clean; additive (r1/r2 unchanged). **Cord r4 LANDED `20260813` -- the Cord reads true, the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/cord_true.rye` `@embedFile`s a real `format cord-dag-v1` record (`tools/fixtures/cord_dag.bron`, produced reproducibly by `mycelium/cord_fixture_gen.rye`), parses it through r3, **rebuilds the Dag under r1's own `add`** (every signature re-verified, every parent already held), commits, and folds the supply through the real Mycelium law, reporting **blocks - supply - stars**. The witness cross-checks that triple against `tools/fixtures/cord_dag_truth.awk` -- a signature-verifying rebuild+fold and a bare arithmetic scan both give **`4 700 1`** (two tools, one answer), so the agreed supply can never drift from a record a keeper adds up by hand. Genuineness: the fixture is rendered in agreed order, so re-committing reproduces the file's order (identity permutation -- else `OrderNotCanonical`); re-rendering reproduces the embedded bytes exactly; the generator re-emits byte-identical bytes (`REPRODUCIBLE`); a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. GREEN first pass; witness `tools/mycelium_cord_true_witness.rish` GREEN (re-runs r3, hence r1/r2), TAME + width clean; additive. **The Mycelium Cord stands complete end to end -- r1 deterministic commit - r2 Byzantine refusal - r3 travels - r4 reads true.** The one remaining Cord rung -- gossiped over the wire -- reaches the serve gate. **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D journey (Kresfa/`.kres` language ecosystem, or Puddle fleet orchestration of the Aurora hypervisor) or another double-seat season's crux, each a self-approved design read first. Breach renames (cairn-first) + deep debride + seed force-push stay Keaton's hand (gates #1/#5).

- **Mycelium fact batching -- COMPLETE `20260813` (r1-r2-r3-r4)** (Season D; design read `active-designing/20260813-043303_mycelium-fact-batching-exploration.md`, self-approved). With the Cord and gossip-convergence arcs closed, the next in-domain Lindy-first crux is the Cord's own named refinement: **one block seals a bounded batch of facts**, the TigerBeetle amortization (studied clean-room) where a block's fixed cost -- one author signature, one node in the agreed order, one round of admission -- buys many facts rather than one. Grown as a **sibling** (`mycelium/cord_batch.rye`), keeping the elder single-fact `cord.rye` and its whole witnessed arc a green binary. A `BatchBlock` seals up to `max_facts_per_block` (16) facts under one author signature; the canonical block bytes bind each fact's own signature, so a tampered fact anywhere breaks the block signature at `add_batch`; `commit_batch` reuses the elder's exact topological rule (round asc - author key - block hash); `committed_batch_log` flattens committed batches to the single fold stream. **The crux on metal:** the same four facts, folded one-per-block across the elder Cord and packed as two batched blocks (round-0 genesis pair - round-1 tax+reserve), yield **byte-identical** issued/taxed/supply/star_count (1000/300/700/1) -- batching is a throughput transform, never a semantic one; the supply never notices how facts were packed. Refusals hold whole: `BatchFull` (mint 17), `EmptyBatch` (mint 0), a tampered fact (`BadSignature`), a dangling parent, a duplicate -- the Dag never grows on a fault. Witness `tools/mycelium_cord_batch_witness.rish` GREEN (elder Cord + Mycelium fold both re-run GREEN at its door), TAME + width clean. Additive -- composes `cord`+`fold` public API only; demo seeds, no key/funds/network/custody; a batched Cord gossiped over Comlink reaches the serve gate. **r2 LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/cord_batch_bron.rye` -- the batched Dag travels as a `format cord-batch-v1` record (per block a `block` line, one `fact` line per batched fact, then `parent` lines); the recovered Dag re-admitted under `add_batch` commits to the **byte-identical order** and folds the identical supply (700, one star), re-rendering is a fixed point, a tampered fact refuses `BadSignature` on rebuild, and a wrong header - unknown field - fact-before-block - empty batch each refuse. Fixed a real red in-round -- a stack overflow from returning the 1.77 MB `BatchDag` by value -> `seat`/`rebuild` now write through an out-pointer (`max_record` shrank to 16384). Witness `tools/mycelium_cord_batch_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (r1 + elder cord-bron re-run GREEN), TAME + width clean. **r3 LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/cord_batch_bench.rye` -- a fixed 32-fact corpus arranged one-per-block (32 blocks) and batched into full 16-fact batches (2 blocks) folds to the identical supply (320); Two Rooms honesty -- asserts only the exact (identical supply - block counts), **reports** the amortization (`loom ... blocks_single=32 blocks_batched=2 block_ratio_x10=160`, a measured 16x fewer block signatures), never an asserted invariant; a wall-time benchmark stays a later loom round. Witness `tools/mycelium_cord_batch_bench_witness.rish` GREEN (r1 + r2 re-run GREEN), TAME + width clean. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/cord_batch_true.rye` `@embedFile`s a reproducibly-generated real `format cord-batch-v1` record (`tools/fixtures/cord_batch_dag.bron`, via `mycelium/cord_batch_fixture_gen.rye` + a `mycelium/corpora/` symlink), parses it through r2, rebuilds under `add_batch` (every signature re-verified), commits (identity permutation asserted), folds, and reports blocks-supply-stars; the witness cross-checks that triple against `tools/fixtures/cord_batch_truth.awk` -- **app == awk, `2 700 1`** (two tools, one answer). Re-render is a fixed point, the generator is `REPRODUCIBLE`, a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_cord_batch_true_witness.rish` GREEN (r2 + r3 re-run GREEN), TAME + width clean. **The Mycelium fact-batching arc stands complete end to end -- r1 folds identical - r2 travels - r3 throughput win - r4 reads true. Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D journey (Kresfa/`.kres`, Puddle) or another double-seat season's crux, each a self-approved design read first. The **supersede-the-core** ruling (evolving `cord.Block` in place, a module-seam decision) and breach renames (cairn-first) + deep debride + seed force-push stay Keaton's hand (gates #1/#5).
- **Mycelium Knot -- OPENED `20260813` (r1)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-050903_mycelium-cord-knot-checkpoint-exploration.md`). With the Cord, gossip-convergence, and fact-batching arcs all closed, the next Lindy-first crux on the Mycelium road is the one durable capability the Cord still lacked: its `Dag` is statically allocated (`cord_max_blocks` 256) -- a TigerBeetle feature -- yet a bound reached is a mesh that stops. Every long-lived deterministic state machine checkpoints: fold the committed past into a summary, prune it, continue from the summary as if from genesis; without it the bounded Dag is a demo, with it the same Dag runs forever. Named the **Knot** (`comlink-tendency`: a knot ties off a length of cord and begins the next; zero collisions grepped), grown as a **sibling** `mycelium/cord_knot.rye` keeping `cord.rye`+`fold.rye` green binaries. A `Knot` seals a committed Cord's `fold.FoldState` (issued - taxed - every reserved star) under a streaming SHA-256 digest -- **no signature** (a Knot is a deterministic function of the committed Dag; the digest is integrity-in-travel, never authority; Kumara still signs each fact, the Cord still orders, the fold still decides supply). `seal_dag` commits+flattens+folds+seals; `continue_verified` folds a next-epoch log onto a verified Knot. **The crux on metal:** a prefix Dag sealed to a Knot, a fresh suffix epoch continued onto it, folds to the **byte-identical supply** (1000/300/700, one star `vega`) as the whole history from empty -- a deep `states_match` over issued-taxed-the whole star set. Both boundaries hold (the Knot at genesis - the full-prefix Knot). **Every fold invariant crosses the cut** (because `fold_fact` carries the law in the state it mutates): a corrupted Knot refuses `KnotCorrupt`, a suffix tax 800 past a checkpointed supply 700 refuses `Overdraw`, a suffix star already sealed refuses `StarTaken` -- the cross-cut facts built raw (append_fact probe-folds from empty and would itself refuse them). One red caught: `append_fact` returns a `FoldState`, not void. Witness `tools/mycelium_cord_knot_witness.rish` GREEN (elder Cord + Mycelium fold both re-run GREEN at its door), TAME + width clean. Additive -- composes `cord`+`fold` public API only; demo seeds, no key/funds/network/custody; a **quorum-agreed** checkpoint (a node pruning on a checkpoint it did not compute) reaches the Comlink-served serve gate, Keaton's hand. **r2 LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/cord_knot_bron.rye` -- a Knot travels as a `format cord-knot-v1` record (issued - taxed - one hex `star` line per reserved star - digest hex), `render`/`parse` (through an out-pointer, the batch-bron stack-safety idiom). The crux: render -> parse recovers a Knot that verifies and carries the **byte-identical** sealed supply (700, one star), continues to the identical supply as the original (a fresh 300 issue onto each -> both issued 1300), and re-rendering is a **fixed point**. A tampered star breaks the digest -> `continue_verified` refuses `KnotCorrupt` (a tampered checkpoint never crosses as trusted); bad header - unknown field - bad hex each refuse; over-full star set refuses `TooManyStars` (max 64). GREEN first pass; witness `tools/mycelium_cord_knot_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (r1 Knot re-runs GREEN at its door), TAME + width clean. **r3 LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/cord_knot_bench.rye` -- checkpointing is a genuine bound win, measured. The ceiling is real (a fresh Dag filled to `cord_max_blocks` 256 refuses the next block `DagFull`); a checkpoint chain of 5 windows of 64 blocks folds **320 facts past the ceiling** (each epoch a bounded Dag sealed to a Knot then pruned, the next continuing onto it) to the **byte-identical** whole-history supply (320), the **live Dag never exceeding 64**. Two Rooms: asserts only the exact facts, reports the amortization as a `loom` line (`total_facts=320 dag_ceiling=256 max_live_blocks=64 windows=5 live_ratio_x10=50 would_overflow=yes` -- 5.0x fewer live blocks, which the ceiling makes impossible not merely costly); wall-time stays a later round. GREEN first pass; witness `tools/mycelium_cord_knot_bench_witness.rish` GREEN (r1 + r2 re-run GREEN at its door), TAME + width clean. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/cord_knot_true.rye` `@embedFile`s a reproducibly-generated real `format cord-knot-v1` record (`tools/fixtures/cord_knot.bron`, via `mycelium/cord_knot_fixture_gen.rye` + a `mycelium/corpora/` symlink), parses it through r2, **verifies the recovered Knot against its own digest**, and reports supply-stars; the witness cross-checks that pair against `tools/fixtures/cord_knot_truth.awk` -- **app == awk, `700 1`** (two tools, one answer). The record verifies, re-renders to the embedded bytes exactly, the recovered Knot continues to a lawful supply (fresh 300 issue -> 1300), the generator is `REPRODUCIBLE`, a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_cord_knot_true_witness.rish` GREEN (r2 + r3 re-run GREEN at its door), TAME + width clean. **The Mycelium checkpointing arc stands complete end to end -- r1 fold identical - r2 travels - r3 bound win - r4 reads true** (the fourth closed 4-round Mycelium arc, after Cord - gossip convergence - fact batching). **Next agent-doable crux:** the supersede-the-core ruling (evolving `cord.Block` in place, a module-seam decision reserved for Keaton), or a fresh Season D journey (Kresfa/`.kres`, Puddle) / another double-seat season's crux, each a self-approved design read first. Breach renames (cairn-first) + deep debride + seed force-push stay Keaton's hand (gates #1/#5).
- **Mycelium contested-verdict arcs -- the Tenure + the Till `20260813`** (Season D). **Tenure COMPLETE** (`mycelium/tenure*.rye`; design `20260813-060935_...`) -- a contested **name** decided by the agreed order: the first reservation in the Cord's total order holds it, a later one is a **lawful no-op** loss, corruption refuses whole (r1 verdict - r2 travels - r3 across a Knot - r4 reads a real fixture true, app == awk). **Till OPENED -- r1 LANDED** (`mycelium/till.rye` + `tools/mycelium_till_witness.rish` GREEN; self-approved design `active-designing/20260813-064129_...`) -- the **double-spend** twin, the oldest reason a value ledger wants consensus (improving on TigerBeetle/Mysticeti). The fund model is a **global supply** (`fold.rye` folds `issued - taxed`), so the truest double-spend is a **supply-exhaustion race**: two keepers each draw 700 from a treasury of 1000 -- each affordable alone, not both. `till.resolve` walks the committed order through `fold.fold_fact`; an accepted spend records a **winning Draw**, an `Overdraw` with amount **<= issued-so-far** (coins once existed -- an earlier draw took them) records a lawful-no-op **spent-out loss**, while an `Overdraw` **> issued-so-far** (never-could-pay) - a broken signature - a `StarTaken` name-contest (the Tenure's verdict, not the Till's) each **refuse whole**. Deterministic across three arrival orders (drew 2 - short 1 - supply folds once to 200 - total lost 700). **r2 LANDED** (`mycelium/till_bron.rye` + `tools/mycelium_till_bron_witness.rish` GREEN) -- the coin verdict travels as a `format till-v1` record (`supply` - `drew <pk> <amt> <pos> <round>` - `short <pk> <amt> <pos> <issued-at>`, keyed by signer), render->parse recovers the identical draws/loss/supply, re-render is a fixed point; six refusals whole (bad header - unknown field - bad hex - broken supply law - missing supply - over-long). Composes `cord`+`fold`(+`till`) public API only; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. **r3 LANDED** (`mycelium/till_knot.rye` + `tools/mycelium_till_knot_witness.rish` GREEN) -- the verdict reads across a **Knot** (a `TillKnot` pairs the sealed `FoldState` with the prefix coin map; `continue_till` walks the suffix seeded by the sealed supply): a treasury of 1000 pays two prefix draws sealed at supply 200, a suffix 700-draw finds only 200 yet the sealed issued is 1000 -> a **spent-out loss across the cut**; `verdicts_match` proves the full Cord and the Knot-continued resolution name the identical draws/loss/coins/supply/loser; corrupt Knot - tampered suffix fact - never-could-pay draw each refuse whole. **r4 LANDED -- Till arc COMPLETE** (`mycelium/till_true.rye` + `till_fixture_gen.rye` + `tools/fixtures/till_dag.bron` + `till_dag_truth.awk` + `tools/mycelium_till_true_witness.rish` GREEN) -- a real reproducible on-disk double-spend record read true: parse via `cord_bron`, rebuild under `cord.add`, resolve, report the spent-out signer-drew-short; **app == awk** (`a09aa5f4... 2 1`, two tools one answer), tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. **The Mycelium Till arc stands complete end to end -- r1 verdict - r2 travels - r3 across a Knot - r4 reads true** (the second closed 4-round contested-verdict arc, after the Tenure). **Purse OPENED -- r1 LANDED `20260813`** (`mycelium/purse.rye` + `tools/mycelium_purse_witness.rish` GREEN; self-approved design `active-designing/20260813-070923_...`) -- the Till's own named horizon, the **truest** double-spend: **one holder spends the same coins twice**, decided **per account** by the agreed order. A transfer rides the Cord with no new fact type -- **sender** = the fact's `signer_pk`, **amount** = its amount, **recipient** = the fact's signed 256-byte `body` (bound to the signature); a genesis credit is `kind_issue` (body = account funded), a transfer `kind_tax` (body = recipient). One **additive** touch to an elder: `fold.verify_fact` (pub) exposes the signature gate `fold_fact` already runs (the shape `cord.verify_block` set) -- fold re-runs GREEN. The Purse folds its own per-account law: the measure is **`received-so-far[holder]`** -- overdraws current balance yet `<= received-so-far` = **lost a race** (lawful no-op spent-out loss); `> received-so-far` = **never could pay** (refuse whole). **The new invariant a per-account model earns -- conservation:** no coin made or destroyed by a move, so `balances_sum` == `total_issued`, asserted at the resolve postcondition. Crux: genesis credits 100 to Alice; Alice signs two 100-transfers (to Bob, to Carol) same round -- a real equivocation `cord.add` admits as two distinct-hash blocks; `commit` tie-breaks by hash, one moves the coins, the other faces an emptied purse. Winner 100 - loser's recipient 0 - Alice 0 (spent once), the loss lawful (100<=received 100), conservation holds, `purses_equal` across both arrivals; `NeverPaid` (500>received) - `IdentityRefused` (tampered sig) - `StarReserve` (name reservation, honest to own resource) - `Malformed` (non-key recipient) each refuse whole. Additive -- composes `cord`+`fold`+`kumara` public API only; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. **Season D's third contested-verdict arc opens (Tenure names - Till supply - Purse accounts), the first with a conservation law. r2 LANDED `20260813`** (`mycelium/purse_bron.rye` + `tools/mycelium_purse_bron_witness.rish` GREEN) -- the per-account verdict travels as a `format purse-v1` record (`issued <total>` - `holds <pk> <bal>` - `moved <from> <to> <amt> <pos> <round>` - `short <from> <amt> <pos> <recv-at>`), render->parse recovers the identical balances/counts/totals, re-render is a fixed point; **conservation travels as the record's own law** (balances must sum to `issued` or `BadInt`), six malformed records refuse whole. **r3 LANDED `20260813`** (`mycelium/purse_knot.rye` + `tools/mycelium_purse_knot_witness.rish` GREEN; self-approved design `active-designing/20260813-072541_...`) -- the per-account verdict reads **across a Knot**, mirroring `till_knot` carried from one shared drawer to many private purses. A `PurseKnot` pairs the integrity-sealed global supply (`cord_knot.seal_dag`, whose `issued` equals the prefix's `total_issued`) with the prefix's per-account map the FoldState omits; `continue_purse` verifies the Knot, seeds a Purse with the prefix balances, and walks the live suffix through the shared `purse.fold_transfer` step. The crux is truer across a cut than within one round -- Alice is credited 100 and transfers it to Bob **inside the sealed prefix**, then signs a fresh 100 to Carol in the suffix; her purse emptied in the checkpointed past, so the suffix transfer comes up **short** (lawful no-op, Carol paid nothing, conservation whole). `purses_match` proves the full Cord and the Knot-continued verdict never drift (moves - loss - balances - loser); a corrupt Knot (`KnotCorrupt`), a tampered suffix fact (`IdentityRefused`), and a never-could-pay suffix transfer (`NeverPaid`, 500 past the 100 ever credited) each refuse whole. One **additive** touch to an elder: `purse.fold_transfer` extracted as the shared per-fact step `resolve` already ran (resolve re-runs byte-identical GREEN). One red caught in-round: the knot<->verdict `issued` pairing is not digest-bound, so asserting it on untrusted input panicked the corruption test -- replaced with `verify` as the runtime gate (a tampered Knot returns `KnotCorrupt`, never a panic). Witness GREEN, TAME bans clean, width exit 0. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- Purse arc COMPLETE** (`mycelium/purse_true.rye` + `mycelium/purse_fixture_gen.rye` + `tools/fixtures/purse_dag.bron` + `purse_dag_truth.awk` + `tools/mycelium_purse_true_witness.rish` GREEN; self-approved design `active-designing/20260813-073500_...`) -- a real reproducible on-disk per-account double-spend record read true: a demo funder credits Alice 100, Alice -> Bob 100 wins at round 1, Bob -> Dan 40 moves uncontested at round 2, Alice -> Carol 100 comes up short at round 2 (her purse emptied). The app parses via `cord_bron`, rebuilds under `cord.add` (every signature re-verified), resolves through `purse`, and reports the spent-out signer - moved - short; the witness cross-checks that triple against an awk doing **per-account balance accounting in file order** -- **app == awk** (`bc7cbcb5... 2 1`, two tools one answer), the losing transfer unambiguously the round-2 one and the two paying moves touching disjoint accounts so both name Alice. Conservation holds over the real bytes (balances 0/60/40/0 sum to the 100 issued); a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Composes `cord_bron`+`cord`+`purse` public API only (each re-runs GREEN at the door); demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. **The Mycelium Purse arc stands complete end to end -- r1 verdict - r2 travels - r3 across a Knot - r4 reads true** (the third closed 4-round contested-verdict arc, after the Tenure and the Till -- the first carrying a conservation law across all four rungs). **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D journey (Kresfa/`.kres` language ecosystem, or Puddle fleet orchestration of the Aurora hypervisor) or another double-seat season's crux, each a self-approved design read first. Per-account mint authority - a served Purse - breach renames (cairn-first) - deep debride - seed force-push stay Keaton's hand.

- **Mycelium two-phase transfer -- the Pledge `20260813` (r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; design `active-designing/20260813-075049_...`). Season D's **fourth** ledger arc and its first **two-phase transfer** -- coins **reserved** now (a pledge), then **honored** (a post -> the recipient) or **released** (a void -> the pledger), TigerBeetle's signature feature read as the Cord's agreed order. Conservation widens by one column: `balance + reserved` always sums to what genesis issued. The crux is **equivocation** -- a holder signing both a post and a void for one pledge at the same round; the Cord's deterministic order settles it **once** (first resolution wins, the second a lawful no-op), identical across every arrival permutation. `mycelium/pledge*.rye`: r1 verdict - r2 travels (`format pledge-v1`) - r3 across a Knot (a pledge opened in the sealed prefix, honored or released in the live suffix) - r4 reads a real fixture true (app == awk, `55154f42...` Bob honored - posted 1 - open 1). Composes `cord`+`fold`+`kumara` public API only; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody.
- **Mycelium linked atomic chain -- the Braid `20260813` (r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; design `active-designing/20260813-081709_...`, self-approved this session). Season D's **fifth** ledger arc, and the first whose honesty spans a **chain of many facts** -- TigerBeetle's *other* signature (linked events, all-commit-or-all-reverse). A **Braid** (fiber family: Cord - Knot - Braid) is a chain of linked `kind_tax` facts sharing one id, each body `L-id-seq-last-to`, links chained causally on the Cord; when the final link closes the chain `settle_braid` evaluates it as **one atom** over a bounded affordability walk -- every link affords and the whole braid **commits**, or any cannot and the whole braid **reverses** (not one coin moves, not even the earlier links that afford alone). `mycelium/braid*.rye`: **r1** the crux (commit-whole-or-reverse-whole, decided once, arrival-independent, conservation on every path) - **r2** travels (`format braid-v1` -- issued - holds - braid-state - links; conservation as the record's own law) - **r3** across a Knot (a chain **opened** before the cut, its routing tail **settled** in the live suffix -- the honest seam that fold's global law reads `kind_tax` as a *burn* yet the Braid uses it as a *conserving* transfer, so a braid seals only its opening) - **r4** reads a real **reverting** fixture true (app == awk, `ed3234b2...` Dave terminal - committed 0 - rejected 1; the whole atom reversed with not one coin moved). Composes `cord`+`fold`+`kumara` (and `cord_knot`/`cord_bron`) public API only, editing none; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody.
- **Mycelium pending-transfer timeout -- the Lapse `20260813` (design-r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; design `active-designing/20260813-085046_...`, self-approved this session). Season D's **sixth** ledger arc, and TigerBeetle's **third** signature primitive after the two-phase Pledge and the linked Braid: a reservation the order itself lets **expire on time**. A **Lapse** is a Pledge carrying a **deadline** measured in the agreed order's own **position** -- never the wall, the one clock every honest node shares -- and `fold_lapse` ticks the clock **before** it applies each fact (lapsing any open pledge whose `deadline <= pos`, reserved coins home to the pledger, state **lapsed**), so a post the order places at or past the deadline finds the pledge **already lapsed** and is a lawful no-op. The deadline rides **inside** the signed pledge body (`P-id-deadline(8 le)-to`) so it cannot move after the signature; a deadline not strictly after its opening refuses `BadDeadline`. Conservation is the Pledge's law unwidened (a lapse is a void by another cause). `mycelium/lapse*.rye`: **r1** the crux (a late honor loses to expiry, decided once, arrival-independent -- a post & void as round-2 siblings both lapse identically; a never-reached deadline stays open) - **r2** travels (`format lapse-v1` -- the deadline column added, `state_lapsed` admitted, a lapse crossing as a lapse) - **r3** across a Knot (the honest seam new to the Lapse: its deadline is an **absolute** order position, so a `LapseKnot` carries the **epoch base** and continues the clock at `base+i` -- a pledge opened before the cut lapses when the suffix reaches its deadline, unbroken) - **r4** reads a real **lapsed** fixture true (app == awk, `55154f42...` Bob - lapsed 1 - open 1; the awk ticks the same file-order clock, parsing the deadline little-endian). Composes `cord`+`fold`+`kumara` (and `cord_knot`/`cord_bron`) public API only, editing none; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. **The Mycelium season now stands: Cord - gossip - fact-batching - Knot - Tenure - Till - Purse - Pledge - Braid - Lapse.**
- **Mycelium account-statement voice -- the Statement `20260813` (design-r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; design `active-designing/20260813-091851_...`, self-approved this session). The fresh Mycelium journey REMEMBER named next: the ledger's primitives each decide the order, yet a holder still meets a stream of facts, not an answer to the plainest question -- **where do I stand?** A **Statement** is that answer, read as a pure, bounded projection of one account out of a resolved `lapse.Ledger`: its **balance** (spendable) - **reserved** (held in open pledges) - **received** (cumulative inflow), and a bounded list of pledge **lines** (made - received), each naming counterparty - amount - state (open - posted - voided - lapsed). **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/statement.rye` -- **a statement reconciles** (an account's reported reserved equals exactly the sum of its own still-open outbound pledges' amounts, forbidden to drift by assertion) and **statements conserve** (Sigma balance + Sigma reserved across accounts == what genesis issued, unwidened from the Pledge's law). Proven across an open scene (Alice reserves 100 to Bob), a posted scene (reserve returns, Bob holds 100), a lapsed scene (Alice's coins come home, her line reads lapsed), a stranger (a true zero statement, no lines, present=false), and arrival-independence inherited from the ledger (two round-2 permutations, one identical statement). A bounded human render a keeper reads. Composes `cord` + `fold` + `lapse` + `kumara` public API only, editing none (each re-runs GREEN); demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. Witness `tools/mycelium_statement_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r2 travels LANDED:** `mycelium/statement_bron.rye` renders a Statement to a `format statement-v1` record (`account <pk> <balance> <reserved> <received> <present>` - one `line <dir> <counterparty> <amount> <state>` per pledge) and parses it back byte-for-byte (fixed point), the crux being **reconciliation survives the wire** -- the record carries reconciliation as its own law, a parsed present statement whose reserved != its open-made sum refusing `BadReconcile` before it is trusted, so a holder checks r1's arithmetic with no ledger of their own. Alice's 200-across-two-open-made-lines carries whole; a stranger's true zero travels; bad header - unknown field - bad hex - missing account - unknown direction - unknown state - broken reconciliation each refuse. Witness `tools/mycelium_statement_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r3 across a Knot LANDED:** `mycelium/statement_knot.rye` reads one account's line over a `lapse_knot`-continued ledger and proves it **does not drift at the checkpoint** -- the statement over the whole Cord equals the statement over the sealed Knot-plus-suffix (position - lines - reconciliation unbroken); the honest seam it surfaces is that **received accumulates across the epoch base**, not restarts at it (Bob, absent at the seal and credited in the suffix by a far-deadline pledge honored across the cut, reads his whole 100 inflow -- the continuity the Lapse's epoch base keeps and the Statement reads); a Knot corrupted after sealing refuses whole, so no line is read from a tampered checkpoint. Witness `tools/mycelium_statement_knot_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r4 reads true -- the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/statement_true.rye` carries the reader onto a real on-disk ledger record (**reusing the Lapse's own `lapse_dag.bron` fixture** -- a statement is a projection of exactly the ledger the Lapse resolves), parses through `cord_bron`, rebuilds under `cord.add` (re-verifying every signature), resolves through `lapse`, projects the **genesis-funded** account's Statement, and reports `bc7cbcb5... (Alice) - balance 100 - reserved 100 - received 200`; the independent `tools/fixtures/statement_dag_truth.awk` reads the same bytes with per-account **deadline-clock** accounting (the union of the Lapse's clock-tick and the Purse's per-account balances) and names the identical position (**app == awk**), both tools choosing the read account the same principled way -- the genesis-funded one, so no constant picks whose line is read. The statement **reconciles** (reserved 100 == its single open made pledge to Carol, the lapsed pledge to Bob counting toward none) and **conserves** (Sigma balance + reserved == the 200 issued) over the real bytes; the fixture is reproducible and a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_statement_true_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **The Mycelium Statement arc stands complete end to end -- r1 reconciliation crux - r2 travels - r3 across a Knot - r4 reads true.** A **served** statement (a holder fetching their line over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate.

- **Mycelium Voucher -- a signed attestation of position -- `20260813` (design-r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-095216_mycelium-voucher-attested-statement-exploration.md`). With the Statement arc complete, the next Lindy-first crux is the blind spot the whole ledger set names but never fills: the Statement reads a position and `statement_bron` travels it, yet a statement that travels is still only a **reading** -- nothing binds those numbers to the real agreed order, so a holder could hand over any balance and the counterparty's only recourse would be to replay the whole ledger. A **Voucher** fixes it -- **proof of position without replay**: a node reads an account's Statement out of a Dag and signs the position (`balance - reserved - received`) bound to an **order-head** (one SHA-256 digest of the committed block order the reading was taken against, folded over the ordered block addresses so it is stable exactly when the agreed order is). **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/voucher.rye` -- `make_voucher` (from one Dag, so the numbers and the order they bind can't come from two worlds), `verify_voucher` (offline signature check against the record's own node key -- trust the node, no ledger), and `check_against(dag)` (re-derive the order-head and re-read the Statement, confirm every signed field -- a node cannot vouch a false balance to a verifier who holds the blocks, nor swap the order it read against, since the order-head is inside the signature). Proven across open - posted - lapsed scenes; **arrival-independence inherited** -- a voucher made against one round-2 permutation checks against the other (the order-head folds to one digest, the Statement to one position); tamper refuses (flipped balance -> `BadSignature`, wrong node key -> `BadSignature`, an honest voucher for one ledger fails `check_against` a genuinely different -- shorter -- ledger). The order-head fold is the Voucher's own bounded derivation (<= `cord_max_blocks` ordered hashes); composes `cord` + `fold` + `lapse` + `statement` + `kumara` public API only, editing none (each re-runs GREEN); demo node/keeper seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. Witness `tools/mycelium_voucher_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r2 travels LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/voucher_bron.rye` renders a Voucher to a `format voucher-v1` record (`node` - `head` - `account` - `position <balance> <reserved> <received>` - `sig`, each hex where fixed-width) and parses it back byte-for-byte (fixed point), the crux being **the signature survives the wire** -- the record carries its attestation as its own law, a parsed voucher whose signature does not verify against its node key over the bound facts refusing `BadSignature` before it is trusted, so a proof of position cannot be forged or mangled in the crossing; a recipient who holds the blocks can still run r1's `check_against` on the recovered voucher. A tampered position (balance +1) breaks the signature; bad header - unknown field - bad hex - missing field each refuse. Witness `tools/mycelium_voucher_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r3 across a Knot LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/voucher_knot.rye` reads a voucher over a `lapse_knot`-continued ledger and proves **a voucher's position survives a checkpoint** -- a node vouches a position against the whole Cord, and a counterparty who has already checkpointed (sealed the prefix into a Knot, discarded the base blocks, kept only the live suffix) reads the identical vouched position from the Knot-continued ledger (the added `check_position_across` verifies the signature offline then confirms the vouched triple equals the continued reading). Proven where a pledge lapses in the suffix and where a far deadline honors it (**received accumulates across the epoch base**, never restarts). The honest seam: the **order-head is the whole-history fingerprint**, base included, distinct from the prefix-only head, so a party that pruned the base checks the position, not the head (`check_against` needs the whole Dag); a flipped position and a wrong signer match no checkpoint, a corrupt Knot refuses whole. Witness `tools/mycelium_voucher_knot_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r4 reads true -- the arc CLOSES `20260813`:** `mycelium/voucher_true.rye` carries the attestation onto a real on-disk Cord record (**reusing the Lapse's own `lapse_dag.bron` fixture**), parses through `cord_bron`, rebuilds under `cord.add` (re-verifying every signature), makes a voucher over the **genesis-funded** account, and reports the vouched tuple `bc7cbcb5... (Alice) - balance 100 - reserved 100 - received 200`; the independent `tools/fixtures/statement_dag_truth.awk` names the identical position (**app == awk**) -- and the **order-head recomputed** from the rebuilt fixture Dag equals the head the node signed, so the signed order is the fixture's own agreed order. The voucher verifies offline and checks against the ledger it vouches; a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_voucher_true_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **The Mycelium Voucher arc stands complete end to end -- r1 proof-of-position crux - r2 travels - r3 across a Knot - r4 reads true.** A **served** voucher (a holder fetching a node's attestation over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate.

- **Mycelium Chorus -- a quorum of independent Vouchers -- `20260813` (design-r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-102533_mycelium-chorus-quorum-attestation-exploration.md`). With the Voucher arc complete, the next Lindy-first crux is the blind spot the Voucher itself left: a Voucher makes a position provable under **one** node's signature, yet a verifier who does not hold the blocks must simply **trust** that single node read the ledger true -- one node can sign a position it never honestly derived. A **Chorus** fixes it -- **proof of position by quorum**: a bounded set of independent Vouchers for the **same** account position at the **same** order-head, believed only when a threshold of **distinct** nodes agree; the fault-tolerant heart the Mycelium-consensus vision named (improving on TigerBeetle/Mysticeti quorum discipline). **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/chorus.rye` -- a Chorus carries one claim (order-head - account - position) and up to `chorus_max_members` (16) member Vouchers; `open` sets the claim to the true reading, `add` gathers one node's voice, and `verify_chorus(threshold)` folds four laws in order -- every member verifies offline (else `BadSignature`), every member agrees on the one claim (else `Disagreement`), the signers are distinct pairwise (a repeated node -> `DuplicateNode`, so one voice cannot manufacture a quorum), the distinct count meets the threshold (else `BelowThreshold`); `check_against(dag)` re-derives the head and re-reads the Statement once and confirms the claim is true. Proven across open - posted - lapsed scenes; **arrival-independence inherited** (a chorus gathered against one round-2 permutation verifies against the other). Composes `voucher` + `cord` + `fold` + `lapse` + `statement` + `kumara` public API only, editing none (each re-runs GREEN); demo node/keeper seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. Witness `tools/mycelium_chorus_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r2 travels LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/chorus_bron.rye` renders a Chorus to a `format chorus-v1` record -- the claim named once (`head` - `account` - `position` - `threshold`) then one `member <node-hex> <sig-hex>` line per voice -- and parses it back byte-for-byte (fixed point), the crux being **the quorum law survives the wire** -- a parsed Chorus is reconstructed member by member from the shared claim then re-run through `verify_chorus` at the record's own stated threshold, so a thin record (`BelowThreshold`), a padded record (`DuplicateNode`), or a forged member (`BadSignature`) refuses before it is trusted; a bad header - unknown field - missing field - bad hex key each refuse. Witness `tools/mycelium_chorus_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r3 across a Knot LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/chorus_knot.rye` reads a whole quorum over a `lapse_knot`-continued ledger and proves **a quorum's position survives a checkpoint** -- `check_position_across` re-runs the quorum law at the threshold then confirms the one agreed triple equals the Knot-continued reading, so a mesh's word stays believable to a party who pruned the base (proven on a pledge lapsing across the cut and a far-deadline pledge honored in the suffix, received accumulating across the epoch base). The honest seam: the **order-head is the whole-history fingerprint**, base included, distinct in the claim **and in every member**, so no node can silently name a shorter order; a forged member - a thin threshold - a corrupt Knot each refuse. Witness `tools/mycelium_chorus_knot_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r4 reads true -- the arc CLOSES `20260813`:** `mycelium/chorus_true.rye` carries the quorum onto a real on-disk Cord record (**reusing the Lapse's own `lapse_dag.bron` fixture**), rebuilds under `cord.add`, gathers a three-node quorum over the **genesis-funded** account, and reports `bc7cbcb5... - balance 100 - reserved 100 - received 200`; the independent `tools/fixtures/statement_dag_truth.awk` names the identical position (**app == awk**). The quorum is honest to the bytes: believed at threshold three yet refused `BelowThreshold` at four (the distinct count the threshold reads the members present), every member's recomputed order-head matching its signed one, checking against the ledger it vouches; a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_chorus_true_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **The Mycelium Chorus arc stands complete end to end -- r1 quorum crux - r2 travels - r3 across a Knot - r4 reads true. The Mycelium season now stands: Cord - gossip - fact-batching - Knot - Tenure - Till - Purse - Pledge - Braid - Lapse - Statement - Voucher - Chorus.** A **served** chorus (a holder fetching several nodes' attestations over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate. **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D journey / double-seat season's crux, each a self-approved design read first. A served ledger - per-account mint authority - breach renames (cairn-first) - deep debride - seed force-push stay Keaton's hand.
- **Mycelium Muster -- a quorum passes a known validator set -- `20260813` (design-r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-110039_mycelium-muster-known-validator-set-exploration.md`). With the Chorus arc complete, the next Lindy-first crux is the blind spot the Chorus itself left: `verify_chorus` counts **any** distinct keys -- three fresh keypairs pass `verify_chorus(3)` -- and nothing named **which** nodes are legitimate validators (both Cord, admitting any signed block, and Chorus, counting any distinct signer, quietly assumed a validator set never written down). A **Muster** fixes it -- **proof of quorum against a known set**: a bounded, named roll of validator keys with a **Byzantine threshold read from its size** (`n-(n-1)/3`, derived never stored so a tampered roll cannot lower its bar); the keystone every BFT protocol rests on (TigerBeetle's fixed replica set, Mysticeti's committee). **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/muster.rye` -- `enroll` (a validator once; refuse `Duplicate`/`MusterFull`), `is_member`, `byzantine_threshold`, and `pass_muster(muster, chorus)` folding three laws -- the Chorus is internally sound (`verify_chorus`), every distinct voice is enrolled (else `NotMember`, a padded quorum refused whole), the enrolled count meets the threshold (else `BelowQuorum`); the demonstration on metal -- three fresh keys the Chorus alone believes (`verify_chorus(3)` passes) **refuse `pass_muster` NotMember** against a roll they were never enrolled in. Threshold arithmetic proven n=1-3-4-7-10 -> t=1-3-3-5-7. Composes `chorus`+`voucher`+`kumara` public API only, editing none (each re-runs GREEN); roll bounded at `muster_max_members` (16); demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. Witness `tools/mycelium_muster_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r2 travels LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/muster_bron.rye` renders a Muster to a `format muster-v1` record -- one `validator <pk-hex>` line per enrolled key -- and parses it back byte-for-byte (fixed point), the threshold **derived by the reader from the count, never written** (so a roll cannot smuggle a lowered bar across the wire); the crux is **the roll's verdict survives the wire** -- a Chorus judged against a Muster parsed from bytes reaches the identical verdict as against the roll in memory (an enrolled quorum passes, three fresh keys refuse `NotMember`); a bad header - empty roll - unknown field - bad hex key - repeated validator each refuse. Witness `tools/mycelium_muster_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r3 across a Knot LANDED `20260813`:** `mycelium/muster_knot.rye` judges a quorum against a roll over a `lapse_knot`-continued ledger and proves **a roll's verdict survives a checkpoint** -- `pass_muster_across` runs the membership-and-quorum law then confirms the agreed position equals the Knot-continued reading, so a party who kept only the Knot plus its suffix applies the same roll and believes the same enrolled quorum (proven on a pledge lapsing across the cut and a far-deadline pledge honored in the suffix, received accumulating across the epoch base); an outsider quorum refuses `NotMember` and a thin one `BelowQuorum` across the cut exactly as in memory, a corrupt Knot yields no continued ledger. Witness `tools/mycelium_muster_knot_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **r4 reads true -- the arc CLOSES `20260813`:** `mycelium/muster_true.rye` carries the roll onto a real on-disk Cord record (**reusing the Lapse's own `lapse_dag.bron` fixture**), the mesh **being** the validator set -- a real three-node quorum over the **genesis-funded** account passes the roll enrolling exactly that mesh, reporting `bc7cbcb5... - balance 100 - reserved 100 - received 200`; the independent `tools/fixtures/statement_dag_truth.awk` names the identical position (**app == awk**). The roll's verdict is honest to the bytes: three validators - three voices - threshold three (the enrolled count exactly the members present), and a roll of three strangers refuses the same real quorum `NotMember`; a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_muster_true_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean. **The Mycelium Muster arc stands complete end to end -- r1 quorum-against-a-roll crux - r2 travels - r3 across a Knot - r4 reads true. The Mycelium season now stands: Cord - gossip - fact-batching - Knot - Tenure - Till - Purse - Pledge - Braid - Lapse - Statement - Voucher - Chorus - Muster.** A **served** muster (a holder fetching the live validator set over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate.
- **Mycelium Warrant -- a validator set rotates only when the current quorum certifies its successor -- `20260813` (design-r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-112420_mycelium-warrant-validator-set-rotation-exploration.md`). The blind spot the Muster left: the roll is **frozen** -- nothing lets a validator set change, yet every long-lived BFT system must rotate its committee (TigerBeetle's view change, Mysticeti's committee reconfig), facing one trap -- *who authorizes the new roll?* If a fresh set of keys could declare itself the validators, the Muster's guarantee evaporates. **Warrant** fills it: authority is **handed forward under signature, never seized** -- a successor roll is believed only when a **Byzantine quorum of the *previous* roll** signed its content-address (SHA-256 over the canonical `format muster-v1` bytes). **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/warrant.rye` -- `roll_digest`, `Warrant`, `open`/`endorse` (`DuplicateSigner`/`WarrantFull`), and `verify_warrant(warrant, prev, next)` folding five laws -- the digests bind both real rolls (`DigestMismatch`), every endorser is enrolled in *prev* (`NotMember`), endorsers distinct (`DuplicateSigner`), every signature holds over the domain-separated tuple (`BadSignature`), the count meets *prev*'s Byzantine threshold (`BelowQuorum`); **the crux on metal** -- the successor's own three fresh keys, signing their own coronation, refuse `NotMember`: **a set cannot crown itself**. **r2 travels:** `warrant_bron.rye` renders a handoff to `format warrant-v1` (epoch - prev/next digest hex - one `endorse <pk> <sig>` line) and parses back byte-for-byte (fixed point), the recovered certificate reaching the identical verdict offline. **r3 across a Knot:** `warrant_knot.rye` walks a **succession chain** `r0->r1->r2->r3`, each roll authorized by a quorum of the roll before it, and proves it **survives a checkpoint** -- a party holding only a trusted anchor roll forward (base handoffs pruned) reaches the identical head as one who walked from genesis; a broken link refuses whole (`NotMember`/`BelowQuorum`/`DigestMismatch`/`StaleEpoch`). **r4 reads true -- the arc CLOSES:** `warrant_true.rye` proves the signed facts are **true to the bytes** -- the prev/next digests the certificate carries equal a system `sha256sum` of the same canonical roll bytes (two tools, one answer), the endorse count an independent `grep -c` of three; a warrant judged against a roll one validator different refuses `DigestMismatch`. Witnesses `tools/mycelium_warrant{,_bron,_knot,_true}_witness.rish` all GREEN, TAME + width clean; composes `warrant`+`muster`+`muster_bron`+`kumara` public API only, editing none; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. **The Mycelium Warrant arc stands complete end to end -- r1 the succession crux - r2 the wire - r3 the chain across a checkpoint - r4 true to the bytes. The Mycelium season now stands: Cord - gossip - fact-batching - Knot - Tenure - Till - Purse - Pledge - Braid - Lapse - Statement - Voucher - Chorus - Muster - Warrant.** A **served** warrant (a holder fetching the live succession chain over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate. **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D journey / double-seat season's crux, each a self-approved design read first. A served ledger - per-account mint authority - breach renames (cairn-first) - deep debride - seed force-push stay Keaton's hand.

- **Puddle -- a fleet places each world at exactly one host by a rule every node computes alike -- OPENED `20260813` (r1, the berth crux)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-114651_puddle-fleet-placement-exploration.md`). With the Mycelium consensus season standing (Cord...Warrant), the next Lindy-first crux on the whole road is the named-but-unbuilt Season D module **Puddle** -- the fleet-orchestration layer that hosts many sovereign worlds (each a bounded Rye whole with its own Pond + Kumara identity; Aurora is its horizon substrate). The blind spot every arc so far left: they built how nodes *agree* (order - supply - quorum - rotation), never **which host runs which world.** Every fleet faces the trap the Warrant faced with authority -- *who decides the placement?* A central scheduler is a single point of failure and of trust; the leaderless answer this tree keeps is that placement is **derived by everyone, never decided by anyone.** A world's assigned host is its **berth**. **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/puddle.rye` -- a bounded `Fleet` of identity-bearing `Host`s (Ed25519 pk + capacity) and named `World`s; `berth(fleet)` derives the placement by **highest weave-score** (`SHA-256(domain - world-id - host-pk)`, the rendezvous/HRW concept studied clean-room), each world taking the highest-scoring host with room in canonical id order. **The crux on metal:** the same five worlds over three hosts, built in two *opposite* arrival orders (hosts and worlds both reversed), berth to the **byte-identical placement** -- every world lands on the same host public key in both fleets, so two nodes reach one placement from the roster alone without ever speaking. Capacity is honored (each host <= its bound), no world orphaned (total berthed == world count); an **oversubscribed** fleet (worlds past total capacity) refuses `Oversubscribed` **whole** rather than strand one, a fleet of worlds with no hosts refuses `NoHosts`; the build refuses `DuplicateHost` - `DuplicateWorld` - `BadCapacity` - `BadWorldName`. The berth is **derived, never written** (no `set_berth`), and ties (never seen with SHA-256) break on the greater public key, never on insertion index -- so determinism holds even in the impossible case. HRW chosen over hash-mod-count for its **minimal-disruption** property (only a changed host's worlds move) -- which becomes r2's convergence. Witness `tools/mycelium_puddle_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean; composes `kumara` + `tally_copy` public API only, invents no storage; demo host/world seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody (a real Aurora host + provisioning reach gates #2/#4; a served fleet reaches the Comlink-served gate). **r2 LANDED `20260813` -- the fleet heals (convergence with minimal disruption):** `mycelium/puddle_convergence.rye` proves the property highest weave-score was *chosen* for over hash-mod-count -- **a host departs and only its worlds re-berth.** Under generous capacity the berth is exactly pure HRW, so removing one host **moves only the worlds it carried** (onto their next-highest-score survivor) and **leaves every world on a surviving host byte-identical** (its top host still stands); the crux made checkable -- `moved == the departed host's former load`, `moved>0 && stayed>0`, no survivor-tenant moved. The **symmetric join** is minimal too -- a newcomer only *pulls* the worlds it now wins, never reshuffling the hosts already there; and the healed fleet stays arrival-order independent. So a failed node costs **one re-placement, never a migration**. Composes r1 public API only, editing r1 nothing (r1 re-runs GREEN at the door); witness `tools/mycelium_puddle_convergence_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds -- no custody. **r3 LANDED `20260813` -- the fleet travels, and berths the same offline:** `mycelium/puddle_bron.rye` renders a fleet to a `format puddle-fleet-v1` record (one `host <pk-hex> <capacity>` line per host in enrollment order, one `world <id>` line each) and parses back **byte-for-byte** (fixed point). The reader rebuilds through r1's own `add_host`/`add_world`, so the fleet's laws **travel with the bytes** (a record can never rebuild a fleet r1 would refuse). **The crux -- the PLACEMENT survives the wire, not merely the bytes:** the recovered fleet berths to the **byte-identical placement** as the fleet in memory, so two nodes agree on where every world runs by exchanging the roster alone. Purpose-built bounded reader: a wrong header refuses `BadFormat`, an unknown field `UnknownField` (never dropped), a malformed line `BadLine`, a bad hex key `BadHex`, a bad capacity `BadInt`, and a duplicate host/world surfaces r1's own `DuplicateHost`/`DuplicateWorld` on rebuild. Composes `puddle` + `kumara` + `parse_int` + `tally_copy` public API only (r1 re-runs GREEN at the door); witness `tools/mycelium_puddle_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds -- no custody. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- reads true, the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/puddle_true.rye` carries the reader onto a **real** on-disk `format puddle-fleet-v1` record (`tools/fixtures/puddle_fleet.bron`, produced reproducibly by `mycelium/puddle_fleet_fixture_gen.rye`, `@embedFile`-bound through the `mycelium/corpora/` symlink). **The crux -- two tools, one answer:** the app parses the record through r3's reader and reports the roster shape **hosts - worlds - total capacity** (`4 6 16`); the independent `tools/fixtures/puddle_fleet_truth.awk` reads the identical triple from the same bytes (`app.out == awk.out` enforced). Genuineness beyond the triple: the fleet **berths over the real bytes** (every world on a real host within capacity, none orphaned), the record **re-renders to the embedded bytes exactly** (a fixed point -- the fixture is what the Puddle emits, not a hand-mock), the generator re-emits byte-identical bytes (`REPRODUCIBLE`), and a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Composes `puddle` + `puddle_bron` public API only (r3 re-runs GREEN at the door); witness `tools/mycelium_puddle_true_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME + width clean; demo seeds -- no custody. **The Puddle agent-doable arc stands complete end to end -- r1 the berth crux - r2 the fleet heals - r3 the roster travels - r4 reads true.** Season D now carries a fleet-orchestration layer beside its consensus season (Cord...Warrant) and its dev-net (Constel). A served fleet (a node fetching the live roster over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host + provisioning reach gates #2/#4. **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D / double-seat season's journey (Kresfa/`.kres` language is a breach rename gated on Keaton's hand; Puddle could grow a served/Aurora rung at the serve gate) -- each a self-approved design read first.
- **Freight -- a world carries its whole signed ledger to its new berth -- OPENED `20260813` (r1, the freight crux)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-121424_puddle-freight-world-state-migration-exploration.md`). With Puddle's fleet-placement arc standing, the next Lindy-first crux is the seam Puddle left: convergence (r2) proved *placement* moves minimally, yet a placement is only a pointer -- a fleet that reshuffles pointers while dropping or altering the ledgers they name is a bank that reassigns your account and forgets your balance. **Freight** is what a world carries to its berth -- its whole signed Mycelium ledger (a Cord Dag) -- and the durability guarantee a fleet is worthless without: when a host departs and a world re-berths, it arrives reading the **byte-identical account position** it held before. Placement moves; state does not. **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/freight.rye` -- a world's `Position` (balance - reserved - received - order-head digest) is read by `read_position(dag, account)` through `lapse.resolve` + `statement.statement_for` + `voucher.order_head_of`, a **pure function of the ledger bytes** that takes no host as input; so the same read on the sending and receiving host must agree. **The crux on metal:** three worlds each carry a distinct owner-signed ledger (distinct pledge ids -> distinct order-heads); a host departs, the heaviest host's worlds re-berth (Puddle's own heal), and each **moved** world genuinely changes host public key **yet reads the byte-identical position** (order-head + triple, `Position.same`), while a **stayed** world keeps both berth and position. **The teeth -- `receive` rebuilds a moving world's ledger and refuses `FreightTampered`** unless the recomputed order-head equals the freight's advertised head, so a carrier that flips one fact byte is caught (proven: a tampered pledge block refuses, the honest freight accepts and reads true) -- a host cannot serve a silently-rewritten position. **Distinctness:** every world's order-head is its own, so a migration can never cross-wire two worlds' state. The position is **derived, never re-declared** (no `set_position`). Composes `puddle` + `cord` + `lapse` + `statement` + `fold` + `voucher` + `kumara` public API only, editing none (Puddle berth - convergence - Statement all re-run GREEN at the door); demo host/world/keeper seeds -- no key/funds/network, no world provisioned or moved. Witness `tools/mycelium_freight_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **r2 LANDED `20260813` -- the whole fleet's state is conserved across a healing:** `mycelium/freight_conservation.rye` raises the claim from one world to the whole fleet -- a `FleetSum` (balance - reserved - received - worlds) read before a host departure equals the sum read after, where every world's after-position is obtained by **receiving its carried freight on its new berth** (r1's receive path), so conservation is proven *through* the migration; six worlds over three hosts (varied credit/pledge so balance and reserved are both non-trivial), the busiest host departs, and `sum_before.same(sum_after)` -- **a host failure costs zero state**. Every order-head stays pairwise distinct (no cross-wiring), and the law has teeth -- a fleet that dropped the moved worlds reads a strictly smaller sum, short by exactly their state. Witness `tools/mycelium_freight_conservation_witness.rish` GREEN. **r3 LANDED `20260813` -- the freight travels as text:** `mycelium/freight_bron.rye` lets a world's freight cross as a `format freight-v1` manifest (world id - berth host key - ledger order-head - position triple + present); a manifest parsed from bytes reads the **byte-identical position** offline (fixed point), and the teeth tie the light record to r1's heavy proof -- the recovered manifest's advertised head **accepts the world's real ledger blocks** through `freight.receive`, while a head altered in transit makes those same blocks refuse `FreightTampered`. Every one of seven fields is required (missing -> `MissingField`); bad header - unknown field - malformed line - bad hex head - bad amount - bad present flag each refuse. Witness `tools/mycelium_freight_bron_witness.rish` GREEN. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- reads true, the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/freight_true.rye` carries the reader onto a **real** on-disk `format freight-v1` record (`tools/fixtures/freight_manifest.bron`, produced reproducibly by `mycelium/freight_fixture_gen.rye`, `@embedFile`-bound through the `mycelium/corpora/` symlink). Two crux checks: **two tools, one answer** -- the app's position triple (balance - reserved - received) equals `freight_manifest_truth.awk`'s reading (`60 40 100`, all three nonzero); and **the record is the freight of a real ledger** -- the app rebuilds the world's ledger from the fixture's own seeds, confirms the parsed position and order-head match byte for byte, and the advertised head accepts the real blocks through `receive`. The record re-renders to the embedded bytes (a fixed point), the generator is `REPRODUCIBLE`, and a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_freight_true_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **The Freight journey stands complete end to end -- r1 the migration crux - r2 conservation - r3 travels - r4 reads true.** Season D now carries a world's *state* across a re-berth, closing the durability seam Puddle left. A served migration (a node fetching a moving world's freight over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host + provisioning reach gates #2/#4. **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D / double-seat season's journey, each a self-approved design read first (breach renames are cairn-first; deep debride + seed force-push stay Keaton's hand).
- **Portage -- value carried across two worlds, atomic on both ledgers or neither -- OPENED `20260813` (r1, the cross-world crux)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-124159_mycelium-portage-cross-world-transfer-exploration.md`). Freight moved a world's whole state across a re-berth, yet every Mycelium coin so far still lived and died inside ONE Cord. The highest-value still-tractable crux on the whole road -- the one the double-seat vision names as beating **TigerBeetle/Mysticeti** -- is value crossing **between two worlds** (shards) with no shared total order, atomic on both ledgers or neither: cross-shard settlement. **Portage** is that crossing (the old word for goods carried between two waters that share no channel). It binds its two halves by a content **digest** -- SHA-256 over `id-amount-from-to_world-to_account` -- so a receiving world credits only a digest it can verify against the sending world's burn; the decision is a **pure function of both ledgers' bytes**, no correspondent trusted (the same insight Freight used for migration, now for transfer). **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/portage.rye` -- its OWN two-world fold law `resolve_world(dag, verify)` (mirroring how Pledge wrote its own over the shared signed facts, since lapse/statement know only P/H/V): a signed portage-**out** (`O-id-world-to`) debits the signer on World A and records the world's burn digest; a signed portage-**in** (`I-id-digest-to`) credits the recipient on World B **only when its embedded digest equals the verified burn's**. **The crux on metal:** Alice on A falls by exactly the amount, Bob on B rises by exactly the amount, **the pair conserves** (`left == reached == 40` -- a credit for every debit, double-entry widened across two shards), and the two worlds' order-heads differ (a real crossing, not a no-op). **The teeth:** an in whose digest matches no verified burn refuses **`NoMatchingBurn`** (World B can never conjure value across the gap), a second in for one id refuses **`AlreadyLanded`** (the crossing lands once), a burn beyond the signer's balance refuses **`NeverPaid`** (a debit cannot be conjured either). The credit is **derived from a verified burn, never re-declared** (no `set_balance`). Composes `cord` + `fold` + `voucher` + `kumara` public API only, editing none (the Voucher order-head and the Pledge two-phase transfer both re-run GREEN at the door); demo world/keeper seeds -- no key/funds/network, no value crossed. Witness `tools/mycelium_portage_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **r2 LANDED `20260813` -- a crossing lands on both worlds or neither (atomic all-or-nothing):** `mycelium/portage_atomic.rye` makes the out **two-phase**, the Pledge's atomicity widened from two phases to two shards -- Alice **stages** (balance->reserved, coins held in transit), then **commits** once the in lands on B (reserve leaves circulation, Bob's now) or **reverts** if it never lands (reserve returns to Alice, whole). **The two-outcome law:** a committed source pairs with a landed in (Alice's spendable falls by the amount **and** Bob rises by it -- both moved, `left == reached == 40`); a reverted source pairs with no in (Alice whole **and** Bob unpaid -- neither moved, the two-world sum as if the crossing never began); never a loss (Alice short, Bob unpaid), never a double (Alice whole, Bob paid). **Equivocation decided once:** Alice signs both commit and revert at one round and both arrival permutations name the identical verdict (`sources_equal`), exactly one settled; a staged-but-undecided crossing holds its coins reserved in transit, never lost. Witness `tools/mycelium_portage_atomic_witness.rish` GREEN (Portage r1 - Pledge re-run at the door), TAME bans + width clean. **r3 LANDED `20260813` -- the portage travels as text:** `mycelium/portage_bron.rye` renders a crossing to a `format portage-v1` record (id - from - to_world - to_account - amount - digest - out-head - in-head) and parses it back byte-for-byte (a fixed point); a purpose-built bounded reader accepts a crossing by its record alone -- the digest self-certifies its bound fields, the out-head ties the record to World A's real ledger with a matching burn, the in-head to World B's real ledger crediting the recipient (`40 left A == 40 reached B`); a tampered field or head, and every malformed record, refuse. Composes portage + cord + voucher + kumara public API only.
