Witness `tools/mycelium_portage_bron_witness.rish` GREEN (r1 crux + r2 atomic re-run at the door), TAME bans + width clean. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- reads true (Portage arc closes):** `mycelium/portage_true.rye` reads a real on-disk fixture, its reported value and digest cross-checked against an independent `awk` of the same bytes (`app==awk`, `40 d2bafdd4` -- two tools, one answer); the record is provably the receipt of a real crossing whose two heads accept both rebuilt ledgers, its fixture reproducible, its record a fixed point; a tampered header refuses. Witness `tools/mycelium_portage_true_witness.rish` GREEN. **The Portage agent-doable arc -- r1 cross-world - r2 atomic - r3 travels - r4 reads true -- stands complete end to end.** A **served** portage (World B fetching World A's out-fact, or A learning of B's in, over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host for either world reaches gates #2/#4.

- **Constel -- a named, reproducible dev-net constellation reaches quorum -- OPENED `20260813` (r1, the named-quorum crux)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-132009_mycelium-constel-named-dev-net-exploration.md`). Every consensus witness before this conjured its nodes inline (`seeded(0x11)`...) -- anonymous keypairs made up for one proof and forgotten; no round gave the dev-net a **name and a roster**. **Constel** is the named test constellation -- the sovereign-lane echo of elder Urbit's fake-galaxy dev networks, run from inside the jailed pier. Its crux: a ship's identity is a **pure function of its name** (`seed = SHA-256(name)`), so the whole constellation -- every key, the Muster it seats, the quorum it reaches -- is determined by the list of names alone. **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/constel.rye` -- `boot(names)` seats each named ship with `keypair_from_seed(SHA-256(name))`, `into_muster` enrolls every ship, and `reach_quorum` opens a Chorus, gathers every ship's voucher, and passes the constellation's own Muster. **On metal:** a five-ship roster boots to **byte-identical keys** twice (reproducible, distinct ships), seats as a Muster (Byzantine threshold **4**), and reaches quorum on the account's **true order-head** (`== voucher.order_head_of`, not a claim). **The teeth:** a stranger ship (a name not seated) refuses **`NotMember`** (an outsider never counts), a quorum short of the threshold refuses **`BelowQuorum`** (a thin quorum is not believed). Identity is derived from the name, never declared (no `set_key`). **Naming law seated this round** -- the first Constel roster (`xnvethqua` - `kwelmirran` - `xzobrilth` - `murrqaveln` - `xwendakosh`), single tokens under the always-on placeholder-ship-names rule (no `~`, length != 3/6/12, never a valid @p), siloed dev fixtures. Composes `kumara`+`cord`+`fold`+`voucher`+`chorus`+`muster` public API only, editing none (Chorus + Muster re-run GREEN at the door); demo seeds -- no key/funds/network, no real value. Witness `tools/mycelium_constel_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **r2 LANDED `20260813` -- a constellation tolerates f down, the (f+1)th honestly fails:** `mycelium/constel_depart.rye` -- the Muster stays the full roster (every ship enrolled whether up or not), only a subset is up and vouches; a five-ship constellation tolerates **1 down** (4 up still meets threshold 4) and **honestly fails `BelowQuorum` at 2 down**, keeping exactly the promise its arithmetic makes (`n - threshold == f`); a permanently smaller roster re-derives its own lower threshold and still agrees. Composes `constel`+`chorus`+`muster`+`voucher` public API only (r1 re-runs GREEN at the door; r1's `demo_genesis`/`seeded` made `pub`, additive). Witness `tools/mycelium_constel_depart_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **r3 LANDED `20260813` -- a named constellation travels, self-certifying:** `mycelium/constel_bron.rye` renders the roster to a `format constel-v1` record (one `ship <name> <pk-hex>` line each) and parses it back byte-for-byte (a fixed point); because a ship's key is a pure function of its name, the record is **self-certifying** -- `parse` re-derives each key from its name and refuses **`PkMismatch`** unless the carried key matches (a tampered key cannot ride a real name). **The crux:** a keeper boots the identical constellation from the record alone -- the recovered roster reaches the **byte-identical quorum head** as the one in memory (the Dag's true order-head). A bad header - unknown field each refuse. Composes `constel`+`chorus`+`muster`+`voucher` public API only (r1 re-runs GREEN at the door). Witness `tools/mycelium_constel_bron_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **r4 LANDED `20260813` -- reads true (Constel arc closes):** `mycelium/constel_true.rye` reads a real on-disk `format constel-v1` fixture (`tools/fixtures/constel_roster.bron`, produced reproducibly by `constel_fixture_gen.rye`, `@embedFile`-bound through `mycelium/corpora/`). **Two tools, one answer:** the app derives the roster shape (`<count> <firstkey8>`) **independently of the record text** -- by booting the constellation from its ship names (each key a pure function of its name) -- and reports `5 97f92882`, cross-checked against `constel_roster_truth.awk`'s reading of the same bytes. **The record is the receipt of the real constellation** -- every carried key self-certifies against its name, the parsed roster matches the name-booted one name-for-name and key-for-key, and it reaches quorum on a demo Dag's true order-head; the record re-renders to the embedded bytes (a fixed point), the generator re-emits byte-identical (`REPRODUCIBLE`), a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witness `tools/mycelium_constel_true_witness.rish` GREEN. **The Constel agent-doable arc -- r1 named-quorum crux - r2 fault tolerance - r3 travels self-certifying - r4 reads true -- stands complete end to end.** A **served** constellation (ships attesting over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host for any ship reaches gates #2/#4.. A **served** constellation (ships attesting over Comlink) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host for any ship reaches gates #2/#4.
- **Testament -- a named constellation's verdict travels as an offline certificate -- `20260813` (r1-r2-r3-r4 COMPLETE)** (Season D; self-approved design read `active-designing/20260813-135126_mycelium-testament-named-quorum-certificate-exploration.md`). The Constel quorum lived and died **in one process** -- nothing carried the constellation's belief out. `chorus_bron` already lets a bare quorum travel and re-verify at a **stated** threshold, yet it carries no roster, so it cannot tell an enrolled ship from a stranger with a valid signature. A **Testament** closes exactly that gap: a portable certificate that *this named constellation, by Byzantine quorum, certified this reading*, verifiable **offline** holding only the certificate and the roster **names**. **r1 the crux:** `mycelium/testament.rye` seals a reading into a certificate carrying the roster names + the sealed Chorus; made **with** the ledger, verified **without** it -- `verify` boots the exact roster from the names alone (each key `SHA-256(name)`), seats the Muster, **derives** the Byzantine threshold from the roster's size, and passes the seal with no Dag. **The teeth (the named binding a bare Chorus cannot enforce):** a stranger ship's valid, agreeing voucher refuses **`NotMember`**, a thin quorum **`BelowQuorum`**, a one-byte name tamper re-boots a different key -> **`NotMember`**. **r2 LANDED -- fault tolerance inside the certificate:** `mycelium/testament_fault.rye` -- a Testament sealed with `f` ships down still names the **whole** roster, so `verify` boots the full constellation, derives the full threshold, and believes the survivors from the names alone (five ships, threshold 4, f 1: one down still meets four); one more down refuses **`BelowQuorum`** at the honest edge; a permanently smaller roster re-derives its own lower threshold and is believed. **r3 LANDED -- the certificate travels, self-proving:** `mycelium/testament_bron.rye` renders to a `format testament-v1` record (one `ship <name>` line each -- the **name only**, key derived; the shared claim once; one `member <node> <sig>` line per voice) and parses back byte-for-byte (a fixed point). Unlike a `chorus-v1` record it states **no threshold** -- `parse` boots the roster, derives the threshold, and re-runs `verify`, so the named binding travels: a stranger's voice refuses `NotMember`, a thin quorum `BelowQuorum`, a tampered ship name `NotMember`, now across the wire. **r4 LANDED -- reads true, the arc CLOSES:** `mycelium/testament_true.rye` reads a **real** on-disk fixture (`tools/fixtures/testament_cert.bron`, produced reproducibly by `mycelium/testament_fixture_gen.rye`, `@embedFile`-bound through `mycelium/corpora/`). **Two tools, one answer:** the app derives the shape (`<ship_count> <head8>`) **independently of the record text** -- ship count by booting the roster from names, head by rebuilding the demo Dag -- reporting `5 c2d99dd2`, cross-checked against `testament_cert_truth.awk`; the certificate self-proves on parse, the parsed roster matches the booted one name-for-name, the sealed head is the demo Dag's true order-head, the record is a fixed point, the fixture `REPRODUCIBLE`, a tampered header refuses `BadFormat`. Witnesses `tools/mycelium_testament{,_fault,_bron,_true}_witness.rish` all GREEN, TAME + width clean; composes `testament`+`constel`+`chorus`+`muster`+`voucher` public API only, editing none; demo seeds -- no key/funds/network/custody. **The Testament arc stands complete end to end -- r1 named-quorum crux - r2 fault tolerance in the certificate - r3 travels self-proving - r4 reads true.** A named constellation's verdict is now a portable, self-proving certificate a keeper reads by hand -- the Lindy artifact the Mycelium consensus season built toward. A **served** testament (ships attesting over Comlink rather than in one process) reaches the Comlink-served gate; a real Aurora host for any ship reaches gates #2/#4. **Next agent-doable crux:** a fresh Season D / double-seat season's journey, each a self-approved design read first (breach renames stay cairn-first; deep debride + seed force-push stay Keaton's hand).

Length budget truncates (length stop); disallowed model - unknown op (no fake answer) - zero budget each refuse. Additive (browser + counting voice stay their own green binaries); witness `tools/buhr_graph_reading_query_witness.rish` GREEN (4 welcomes + 3 unwelcomes + browser re'run), TAME + width clean. **BUHR Journey 6 stands complete end to end -- read - track'a'live'edit - gathered'under'host - reading'fold; Surface and Intelligence meet over one graph.** **BUHR Journey 7 OPENED `20260812` -- Tablecloth, the named artifact store:** with Journey 6 complete, the itinerary's blind spot is the equinox's **last unbuilt module** -- **Tablecloth**, the Lexicon's *Brix'facing application database and artifact store* (held apart from the already'witnessed namespace query). A self'approved design round (`active-designing/20260812-142132_buhr-journey7-tablecloth-artifact-store-exploration.md`) opened it Lindy'first crux'first; the crux -- **a human name bound to a content address**, so fetch'by'name returns exactly the bytes whose digest the name pins, a tamper anywhere refusing. **BUHR'J7r1 LANDED `20260812`:** `pond/apps/tablecloth.rye` is the **naming layer over `mantra/beading.rye`'s content'addressed `BeadStore`** -- it invents no storage. `store_artifact` beads content into the shared store (dedup earned: identical content stored once, named twice -- `notes.txt` + `notes-copy.txt` share every bead, 0 new deposits) and binds a name to the whole digest + bead'index recipe; `fetch_artifact` reassembles by name, every bead proving itself by digest and the whole against its recorded address (a tampered bead refuses `DigestMismatch`, an unknown name `UnknownArtifact`, a taken name `NameTaken`). The Brix'facing `format tablecloth-v1` manifest (`artifact <name> <digest> <len>`) round'trips name-address-length field for field; a wrong header refuses `BadManifest`. Migrated the one `parseInt` site to `tally/parse_int.rye` (ratchet 9->8). Additive -- beading stays its own GREEN binary; witness `tools/buhr_tablecloth_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **BUHR'J7r2 LANDED `20260812` -- the manifest as the true index:** `pond/apps/tablecloth_index.rye` `verify_against_store` proves a parsed `format tablecloth-v1` manifest is an **honest index of a live store** -- every published name is held, reassembles (each bead by digest, the whole against its recorded address via `fetch_artifact`), and matches its published **length** and **address** (`tablecloth.address_hex`, added additively with `address_hex_width`). A name the store lacks refuses `UnknownArtifact`, a wrong length `LengthMismatch`, a wrong address `AddressMismatch` -- so a person handed a manifest can check the store satisfies it, artifact for artifact. Additive -- rung one (the naming layer) stays its own GREEN binary under the reader. Witness `tools/buhr_tablecloth_index_witness.rish` GREEN, TAME bans + width clean. **BUHR'J7r3 LANDED `20260812` -- a Brix descriptor declares artifacts the store satisfies:** `pond/apps/tablecloth_brix.rye` `evaluate` makes the Brix'facing half literal -- a `format tablecloth-decl-v1` descriptor (one `require <name>` line per needed artifact) is **evaluated against a live store** into a `format tablecloth-v1` manifest of exactly those declared artifacts, in declared order. The join refuses honestly: a required name the store cannot satisfy refuses the whole evaluation (`Unsatisfied`), never emitting a manifest naming bytes the store lacks; a bad header or empty descriptor refuses `BadDescriptor`. The evaluated manifest then **verifies against the store by rung two**, closing the chain -- a system declares what it needs, the store proves it serves it, the manifest is the audited receipt. A publication resolves to *whose* it is -- `owner_of` returns the point, `place_of` resolves it to its galaxy/star/planet (symmetric with `mandate/keyed.rye`'s `place_of`). ## Card habits

- **kg** -- keep going, next mechanical lap. **check-in** -- pause for Keaton's word / design. **send** -- commit - push both remotes - merge. **remember** -- reprint this card. **align** -- walk the compass, reconcile plan with green witnesses. **molt** -- prep a fossil for shed. **debride** -- remove dead history (Keaton's word). **shred** stays RED until circled. remember != send != kg != align.
- **Vocabulary** -- the tree seats **shape**, not Hoon's *mold*. Prefer **git nib**. One clock: `TZ=America/New_York`.
